Accordingly, spatial location, forecast lead time, and output resolution are introduced as queries, enabling standard hourly forecasts, intermediate lead-time diagnostics, and resolution-controllable outputs within a unified field-evaluation framework. Furthermore, to maintain coherence across flexible field queries, we design spatial-gradient, temporal-difference, and scale-consistency objectives that regularize regional thermal structures, lead-wise evolution, and cross-resolution agreement. Experiments on the Southeast China 0-6 h ERA5-Land benchmark demonstrate that CSTF achieves the best aggregate deterministic skill, including a 17.0 percent reduction in Bias, with global-scope diagnostics further illustrating flexible lead-time and resolution-controllable inference.

</details>


### [89] [VINCENT: Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25841)
*Fan-Sheng Chuang,Xuchen Li,Yujing Bian,Kaixiong Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VINCENT，一个用于药物协同预测的后训练解释框架，通过注意力与梯度信号提取原子对证据、聚合成化学基序，并利用重复局部扰动验证候选基序对，将验证结果反馈以精炼解释，在文献标注子集上达到0.826的平均基序召回率，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药物协同预测中，单一协同分数不足以指导药物组合发现，研究人员还需要了解哪些分子区域共同驱动预测。现有可解释协同模型虽然能暴露原子或亚结构级别的信号，但其解释内置于预测器架构中，且均未在重复扰动下验证跨药物区域分数，也未将证据反馈以精炼解释。因此需要一种化学上连贯、扰动稳定且与预测器行为一致的可靠基序对解释方法。

Method: VINCENT是一个针对固定交互感知协同预测器的后训练框架。它从注意力与梯度信号中提取原子对证据，将原子分组为化学连贯的基序，通过重复局部扰动验证候选基序对，再将验证后的证据反馈以精炼基序分配，从而获得满足化学连贯性、扰动稳定性和预测器行为一致性三个标准的解释。

Result: 在25对文献标注子集上，VINCENT达到0.826的平均基序召回率（95% CI: 0.78-0.87），基线的范围为0.49-0.66。在全部71个测试对上，其验证后的交互分数达到3.36的TP/TN分离度。

Conclusion: 闭环扰动验证比现有替代方法更准确地恢复文献支持的分子区域，同时生成的跨药物交互分数更能反映预测器的行为。VINCENT为药物协同预测提供了一种化学上连贯、扰动稳定且与预测器行为一致的可信解释方案。

Abstract: Drug synergy prediction estimates whether two drugs produce a stronger joint effect than expected from their individual activities. For drug combination discovery, a single synergy score is often not enough: researchers also need to know which molecular regions jointly drive the prediction. We study motif-pair synergy explanation, which identifies pairs of chemically coherent regions, one from each drug, that jointly contribute to predicted synergy. Existing interpretable synergy models expose atom- or substructure-level signals, but their explanations are built into the predictor architecture, and none validates cross-drug region scores under repeated perturbations or feeds that evidence back to refine the explanation. A reliable motif-pair explanation should instead be chemically coherent, perturbation-stable, and aligned with predictor behavior. We introduce VINCENT (Validated Interaction Network for Cross-drug Explanation of Therapeutics), a post-training framework for a fixed interaction-aware synergy predictor. VINCENT extracts atom-pair evidence from attention and gradient signals, groups atoms into chemically coherent motifs, and validates candidate motif pairs through repeated local perturbations. The validated evidence is fed back to refine motif assignments, yielding explanations that satisfy these three criteria. On a 25-pair literature-annotated subset, VINCENT achieves a mean motif recall of 0.826 (95% CI: 0.78-0.87), compared with 0.49-0.66 for baselines. Across all 71 test pairs, its validated interaction scores yield a TP/TN separation of 3.36. These results show that closed-loop perturbation validation recovers literature-supported molecular regions more accurately than existing alternatives while producing cross-drug interaction scores that better reflect predictor behavior.

</details>


### [90] [CEDAR: Controlled and Event-Driven Demand Forecasting via Residual Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25871)
*Junjie Meng,Ranxu Zhang,Zi-an Zhang,Shujun Liu,Xiaoning Qi,Xiaozhou Xu,Yanyong Zhang,Hui Xiong,Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CEDAR是一个两阶段的决策条件化预测框架，用于大规模电商场景下的可控模拟，通过动作交错Transformer学习动作调节的状态转移，并用残差校正模块结合外部事件信号与LLM文本表示修正事件驱动的偏差，在阿里巴巴1688约3200万产品轨迹的真实数据集和在线实验中验证其优于强TSF基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商大规模市场的预测需求已从被动预测转变为评估未来动作序列（如预算调度）下的销售结果。然而现有时间序列预测方法本质上是被动的，即使将运营决策纳入辅助协变量，也只是在历史策略下优化基于相关性的外推，存在自回归惯性并将内生市场演变与决策诱导的转变混淆，导致策略不敏感的展开和不可靠的反事实分析。

Method: 提出CEDAR两阶段框架：Stage I使用Action-Interleaved Transformer学习可控的动作条件化状态转移，支持计划干预下的展开；Stage II使用残差校正模块，利用外部事件信号和LLM辅助文本表示将噪声事件描述与产品上下文对齐，校正事件驱动的偏差。

Result: 基于阿里巴巴1688的大规模真实数据集（约3200万产品轨迹，配对状态-动作序列和对齐事件信号），离线实验和在线受控实验表明CEDAR在模拟准确性上持续优于强TSF基线，并为实际预算规划带来实用收益。

Conclusion: CEDAR通过动作感知的残差分解实现了稳健的决策条件化模拟，有效解决了被动TSF在政策敏感性和反事实分析方面的局限性，为大规模电商平台的规划和决策提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Forecasting in large-scale e-commerce marketplaces is increasingly required to support planning: merchants need to evaluate sales outcomes under future action sequences such as budget schedules, rather than passively predicting what happens next. However, most existing time series forecasting (TSF) approaches remain inherently passive. Even when incorporating operational decisions as auxiliary covariates, they typically optimize for correlation-based extrapolation under historical policies. This design suffers from autoregressive inertia and conflates endogenous market evolution with decision-induced transitions, leading to policy-insensitive rollouts and unreliable counterfactual analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose CEDAR (Controlled and Event-Driven Demand forecasting via Action-aware Residual decomposition), a two-stage framework for robust decision-conditioned simulation. In Stage I, an Action-Interleaved Transformer learns controllable action-conditioned state transitions for rollout under planned interventions. In Stage II, a Residual Correction Module leverages external event signals and LLM-assisted text representations to align noisy event descriptions with product context and correct event-driven deviations. Our study is enabled by a large-scale real-world dataset from Alibaba 1688, comprising approximately 32 million product trajectories with paired state-action sequences and aligned event signals. Extensive offline experiments and online controlled experiments in production demonstrate that CEDAR consistently improves simulation accuracy over strong TSF baselines and delivers practical gains for real-world budget planning.

</details>


### [91] [How Edge of Stability Hinders SCAFFOLD in Federated Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25873)
*Anant Khandelwal,Michael Crawshaw,Mingrui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个理论框架来解释SCAFFOLD算法在联邦学习实践中性能不如FedAvg的原因。作者发现联邦优化中存在"稳定性边缘"(EoS)和渐进尖锐化现象，并且SCAFFOLD在EoS状态下对全局梯度的估计严重退化，从而解释了其理论与实践的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中数据异质性理论上会减慢优化，SCAFFOLD算法被设计为不受数据异质性影响，理论上具有强保证，但在实践中通常不如更简单的FedAvg。作者试图理解这一理论与实践的差距。

Method: 通过大量经验探测实验，在多种架构和超参数下验证FedAvg和SCAFFOLD均存在EoS类动态。测量尖锐度的平衡值（与学习率和数据异质性程度的关系），并分析SCAFFOLD在EoS状态下估计全局梯度的误差与尖锐度之间的相关性。

Result: 发现：1) FedAvg和SCAFFOLD在多种设置下均表现出EoS动态；2) 尖锐度的平衡值与学习率成反比（与GD一致），且受数据异质性影响（但不受本地步数影响）；3) SCAFFOLD在EoS时对全局梯度的估计能力严重退化，尖锐度与梯度估计误差正相关。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD在深度学习场景下的不佳性能源于EoS现象：在高尖锐度下，SCAFFOLD无法可靠估计全局梯度。这为理解联邦优化算法的实际行为提供了新视角，打破了传统上仅从收敛率理论看待该问题的视角。

Abstract: In federated learning, it is well known that heterogeneous data can (in theory) slow down optimization, and much effort has been directed at designing optimization algorithms that are unaffected by data heterogeneity, such as the SCAFFOLD algorithm. Yet, despite strong theoretical guarantees, SCAFFOLD does not usually outperform the much simpler FedAvg in practice. In this work, we propose that this gap is due to the presence of Edge of Stability (EoS) and progressive sharpening in federated optimization, supported by extensive empirical probing. First, we find that EoS-like dynamics occur with both FedAvg and SCAFFOLD under a variety of architectures and hyperparameters. We observe that the equilibrium value of the sharpness is inversely proportional to the learning rate (as in GD), and interestingly, the degree of data heterogeneity (but not the number of local steps) also affects the equilibrium value. Most importantly, we observe that SCAFFOLD's ability to estimate the gradient of the global objective is severely degraded at the EoS, as measured by the correlation between sharpness and SCAFFOLD's error in estimating the global gradient along the optimization trajectory. This suggests a mechanism for SCAFFOLD's lackluster performance in deep learning: with high sharpness at the EoS, SCAFFOLD cannot reliably estimate the global gradient.

</details>


### [92] [A General-Purpose Molecular Foundation Model Transfers Across Diverse Olfactory Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25893)
*Yikun Han,Yi Wang,Neil Mankodi,Stephen Yang,Ambuj Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过在单一嗅觉得分描述基准上微调分子基础模型Uni-Mol2，验证了其无需额外深度学习训练即可在四个互补的下游任务（跨数据集嗅觉得分预测、气味/无气味分类、对映体评估、气味混合物可区分性）中实现迁移学习。该模型在主要基准上达到或超越现有最优嗅觉专用基线，并能区分三维镜像分子，支持"一次训练、多任务迁移"的机器嗅觉范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子基础模型已被广泛应用于分子性质预测，但尚不清楚在单一经典嗅觉预测任务上微调后，是否能够学习到可迁移至多种机器嗅觉问题的通用表征。研究者希望通过实证验证这一关键问题。

Method: 在GS-LF多标签气味描述基准上微调Uni-Mol2模型，然后在不进行任何额外深度学习训练的情况下，在四个互补的下游任务上评估迁移性能：跨数据集嗅觉得分描述预测、气味与无气味分类、对映体评估以及气味混合物可区分性。

Result: 微调后的模型在GS-LF主基准上达到或超越现有最优嗅觉专用基线，并在所有下游评估中表现出一致的迁移能力。对映体分析表明，三维分子表征能够区分镜像分子，而二维图模型本质上无法做到；但准确预测立体化学的感知后果仍是开放挑战。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持机器嗅觉领域"一次训练、跨任务迁移"的范式，表明化学预训练的分子表征为可迁移的嗅觉预测提供了强大基础。

Abstract: Foundation models have transformed molecular property prediction, yet it remains unclear whether a molecular foundation model, fine-tuned on a single canonical olfactory prediction task, can learn representations that transfer across diverse machine olfaction problems. We investigate this question by fine-tuning Uni-Mol2 on the GS-LF benchmark for multi-label odor descriptor prediction and evaluating the resulting model, without additional deep-learning training, on four complementary downstream settings: cross-dataset odor descriptor prediction, odorous-versus-odorless classification, enantiomer evaluation, and odor mixture discriminability. The fine-tuned model matches or exceeds the performance of the state-of-the-art olfaction-specific baseline on the primary GS-LF benchmark and consistently transfers across these downstream evaluations. The enantiomer analysis further shows that three-dimensional molecular representations distinguish mirror-image molecules in a way that two-dimensional graph models fundamentally cannot, although accurately predicting the perceptual consequences of stereochemistry remains an open challenge. Together, these results support a train-once, transfer-across-tasks paradigm for machine olfaction and suggest that chemically pretrained molecular representations provide a strong foundation for transferable olfactory prediction.

</details>


### [93] [Towards A Unified Information Bottleneck Framework for Time Series Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25897)
*Xu Zheng,Zichuan Liu,Zhuomin Chen,Mayur Akewar,Janki Bhimani,Jason Liu,Mo Sha,Jingchao Ni,Wei Cheng,Dongsheng Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的时间序列可解释性框架，将归因解释与反事实解释通过信息瓶颈原理整合到单一目标函数中，利用参数化变换网络构造解释嵌入实例，同时输出忠实的归因和稳定的反事实解释，在合成与真实基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列深度模型解释方法分为归因类和反事实类两大类，但二者被独立研究：归因方法缺乏因果验证，反事实方法受严重不稳定性困扰（产生对抗噪声而非有意义解释）。作者从信息论视角揭示现有解释器易受平凡解与分布偏移影响，亟需统一框架同时解决两类方法的缺陷。

Method: 作者提出一个统一的目标函数，基于信息瓶颈（Information Bottleneck）原理在同一框架内桥接归因推理与反事实推理。该公式显式防止平凡解释和分布外反事实。据此引入模型框架，学习一个参数化变换网络来构造解释嵌入实例：保留的信息产生归因解释，受控的信息移除产生稳定的反事实解释。

Result: 在合成基准和真实世界基准上，与最先进基线方法进行对比。大量定量与定性结果表明该框架在生成忠实归因和稳定反事实解释方面持续优于竞争方法。

Conclusion: 通过信息论视角统一归因与反事实解释是可行且有效的，信息瓶颈约束能够同时解决归因缺乏因果验证和反事实不稳定性这两大问题，为时间序列可解释性提供了一个一致的理论框架。

Abstract: Explaining deep learning models operating on time series data is crucial in various applications that require transparent and interpretable insights into model behavior. {Existing explanation methods generally fall into two categories: attribution-based explanations, which identify the temporal regions most responsible for a prediction, and counterfactual explanations, which reveal how an input should be modified to alter the model's decision.} {Despite valuable insights, these two fields are largely studied independently. This disconnect leaves attribution methods lacking causal validation, while counterfactual methods suffer from severe instability, producing adversarial-like noise instead of meaningful explanations.} In this work, we revisit time-series explainability from an information-theoretic perspective and show that existing explainers are vulnerable to trivial solutions and distributional shifts. To address these limitations, we propose a unified objective function for explainable time series learning that bridges attribution and counterfactual reasoning within a single framework. Building upon the Information Bottleneck principle, our formulation explicitly prevents trivial explanations and out-of-distribution counterfactuals. {Based on this objective function, we introduce {\modelname}, a novel explanation framework that learns a parametric transformation network to construct explanation-embedded instances, where preserved information yields attribution explanations and controlled information removal produces stable counterfactual explanations.} We evaluate {\modelname} on synthetic and real-world benchmarks against state-of-the-art baselines. Extensive quantitative and qualitative results show that {\modelname} consistently outperforms competing methods, yielding faithful attributions and stable counterfactual explanations.

</details>


### [94] [Forecasting Multiple Observables with SCROLL: Score-Trained Uncertainty for Stochastic Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25898)
*Pavel Prochazka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于共享骨干网络、按任务自由路由的自由路由末层信念（free-routed last-layer beliefs）方法，用于随机动力系统的多任务预测。该方法将单元相关的损失缩放吸收进与梯度同步学习的似然参数中，从而避免了多任务中常见的损失权重调优。在齐方差OU过程上，学习到的预测定律能恢复解析核并使规范基线持平；在异方差系统（随机Lorenz-63和真实空气质量数据）上，该信念的输入相关方差在状态和模态任务上取得最优单次运行NLL，校准性能优于或打平被其超越的基线，且计算成本远低于调优网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机动力系统的预测通常需要同时输出多个可观测量的概率分布（如未来状态、阈值事件、模态标签等），而标准多任务方法需要逐任务调节或学习损失权重，这既昂贵又难以决定最优折衷。现有静态基准无法提供计算预测方差的真值，而随机动力系统可以提供可计算的预测方差真值，从而为评估多任务预测方法提供了更可靠的测试平台。

Method: 在共享骨干网络之上，为每个任务构造独立自由路由的末层信念（free-routed last-layer beliefs），以乘积形式组合各观测量的似然。单元相关的损失缩放被吸收进似然参数中，这些参数与网络权重在同一梯度下降过程中联合学习，无需显式调整或学习任务权重。通过在齐方差OU过程、异方差随机Lorenz-63及真实空气质量数据上进行验证。

Result: 在齐方差OU过程上，学到的方法恢复了解析核，且与规范基线持平；在异方差系统上，该方法在状态和模态任务上取得最优单次运行NLL，校准性能与NLL被其击败的基准持平，而计算成本仅为调优网格的一部分。在真实数据上，状态边际在五个滚动时间起点上保持一致。

Conclusion: 自由路由末层信念方法通过将损失缩放的调整转移到似然参数的学习中，有效规避了多任务权重的调优难题，并在随机动力系统的多任务预测上取得了理论预期与实际效果的一致性，验证了该方法的有效性、校准性以及计算效率。

Abstract: Forecasting a stochastic dynamical system rarely means a single number: one wants several observables---future state, threshold event, regime label---each with its own likelihood. Standard multi-task recipes balance per-task losses, tuned or learned. We instead compose the observables' likelihoods in per-task free-routed last-layer beliefs on a shared backbone; this absorbs unit-dependent loss scaling into likelihood parameters learned in the same gradient pass. Stochastic dynamics supply what static benchmarks cannot: computable ground truth for the predictive variance. Results land where theory puts them: on the well-specified, homoscedastic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process the learned predictive law recovers the analytic kernel and correctly specified baselines tie. On heteroscedastic systems (stochastic Lorenz-63, real air-quality data) the belief's input-dependent variance separates: best single-run NLL on the state and regime tasks, calibration matched only by arms whose NLL it beats, at a fraction of the tuned grids' cost. On the real series the state margin holds across five rolling origins.

</details>


### [95] [Quantum-Inspired Modeling of Driving Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25907)
*Mohammad Elayan,Omid Armantalab,Wissam Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子启发的驾驶员行为表征框架，将每个驾驶员编码为演化的密度矩阵，统一表征行为不确定性、时间演化和情境依赖性。该框架在I-24 MOTION数据集上无监督训练，恢复出自由流、过渡和拥堵三种可解释驾驶模式，并复现基本图和滞回环等宏观交通现象，同时可为经典跟驰模型提供情境依赖参数，并支持自动驾驶车辆的实时行为感知与短时预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶员行为具有异质性、情境依赖性和时变性，但大多数现有模型预先固定行为变量的交互方式和机制，无法充分捕捉超出固定形式的行为（被当作噪声吸收）；而过度灵活的模型又丧失可解释性。作者希望构建一个既连续、概率化、情境依赖、历史依赖，又能从数据中学习行为变量间交互的统一样式表征。

Method: 采用量子启发的表征：每个驾驶员被编码为一个演化的密度矩阵，提供行为不确定性、时间演化与情境依赖变化的统一表示。在I-24 MOTION数据集上进行无监督训练，不依赖标签学习驾驶行为表征；该表征被用于恢复驾驶模式轮廓、复现宏观现象，并作为情境依赖参数输入经典跟驰模型及为自动驾驶提供实时行为读取和短时运动预测。

Result: 框架恢复出三种可解释的驾驶轮廓：自由流、过渡和拥堵三个行驶阶段，捕捉了数据中的行为范围和驾驶员在条件变化时在模式间的平滑过渡。该表征复现了已知的宏观现象（与基本图一致并复现滞回环）。同时支持实践应用：为经典跟驰模型提供情境依赖参数，并为自动驾驶车辆提供周围驾驶员的实时行为读取和短时前向预测。作者发布开源工具包。

Conclusion: 该框架表明量子启发的密度矩阵表征能够统一捕捉驾驶员行为的连续、概率、情境依赖和历史依赖特性，兼具可解释性与实际应用价值，指向一种构造上就具有解释性和可信性的交通建模范式。

Abstract: Driver behavior is heterogeneous, context-dependent, and changes over time, and these properties shape the traffic phenomena we observe. Most models, however, fix in advance which behavioral variables interact and how. Behavior outside that form is absorbed as noise, while models flexible enough to capture it tend to lose interpretability. We introduce a quantum-inspired representation of driver behavior that combines properties usually treated separately or in part: it is continuous, probabilistic, context-dependent, history-dependent, and represents interactions among behavioral variables as learned from data. Each driver is encoded as an evolving density matrix, providing a unified representation of behavioral uncertainty, temporal evolution, and context-dependent behavioral variation. Trained without supervision on the I-24 MOTION dataset, the framework recovers three interpretable driving profiles representing three regimes: free flow, transition, and congestion. The profiles capture the behavioral range of the data and the smooth transitions drivers make between regimes as conditions change. The same representation also reproduces known macroscopic phenomena, aligning with the fundamental diagram and reproducing hysteresis loops. We also show how the representation supports practical use: it supplies context-dependent parameters to classical car-following models, and gives an autonomous vehicle a live behavioral read of the surrounding drivers with a short-horizon forecast of their motion. The framework points toward models of traffic that are interpretable and trustworthy by construction. We release an open-source toolkit on GitHub (https://github.com/mselayan/quantum-driver-representation) spanning data processing, training, inference, and analysis.

</details>


### [96] [One Symptom, Three Levers: A Critical Review of On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25936)
*Justin Robert,Raheel Qader*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了"同策略自蒸馏"（On-Policy Self-Distillation, OPSD）方法，它无需额外的大教师模型，而是让模型自身基于特权信息（如参考解、计划或环境反馈）充当教师。文章指出该方法目前主导的失败模式是"崩溃"（collapse）——模型推理路径的逐步收窄，并将其归因于三个控制杠杆：(i)信号应用位置（token权重）、(ii)教师可见信息（特权信息性质）、(iii)信号变化时机（教师动态与引导衰减）。论文聚焦数学推理领域，不报告新实验，贡献在于构建统一术语体系并厘清已解决与仍有争议的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD在早期实验中表现良好，但同策略采样与特权信息不对称导致了推理路径崩溃这一严重退化问题，且不同文献对此现象的命名与描述不一致，缺乏统一分析框架。

Method: 这是一篇综述性论文，采用结构化的杠杆框架（三个维度：信号的应用位置、教师所见信息、信号变化时机）系统性分析崩溃现象，并比较OPSD与传统强化学习/模仿学习方法的异同。

Result: 文章识别出崩溃是OPSD领域的主导性失败模式，特权信息会加剧该问题；提出三个控制杠杆用于诊断和治理崩溃；建立了跨论文的统一术语体系。

Conclusion: OPSD的教师并不比学生更强，只是信息更充分；崩溃并非OPSD特有，但特权信息加剧了它。论文为后续研究提供了结构化分析框架，明确区分了已有定论和仍存争议的问题。

Abstract: On-policy distillation trains a language model on its own generations while a teacher scores them token by token. It combines the dense supervision of imitation learning with the on-policy sampling of reinforcement learning. But it requires a second, larger model to act as teacher. On-Policy Self-Distillation (OPSD) removes that cost. The teacher is the model itself, conditioned on privileged information the student will not have at test time, such as a reference solution, a plan, or environment feedback. The teacher is no stronger than the student, only better informed. Early results were promising, with accuracy comparable to reinforcement learning at a fraction of the generated tokens. But the same asymmetry that produces the signal also biases it. One failure mode now dominates the field: collapse, the progressive narrowing of the set of reasoning paths the model can produce. Collapse is not specific to OPSD, though privileged information aggravates it. This review treats collapse as a symptom governed by three levers: (i) where the signal is applied, that is, how tokens are weighted; (ii) what the teacher is shown, that is, the nature of the privileged information; and (iii) when the signal changes, that is, the teacher's dynamics and the decay of guidance. We restrict our scope to mathematical reasoning, where the method originated and where its failure modes are best documented. We report no new experiments. The contribution is structural: a shared vocabulary for phenomena named differently across papers, and a clear line between what is settled and what is still disputed.

</details>


### [97] [When Pruning Meets Interpretability: Preserving Sparse Autoencoder Robustness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25941)
*Suchit Gupte,Xueru Zhang,Mohammad Mahdi Khalili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret the internal representations of large language models (LLMs), yet their reliability under post-hoc model compression remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of how pruning affects SAE behavior and theoretically show that, for a fixed SAE, its impact is governed by perturbation energy, a covariance-weighted norm. This perspective exposes a key limitation of magnitude pruning: by ignoring activation geometry, it distorts the learned representation space and degrades SAE functionality. Activation-aware methods such as Wanda and SparseGPT, in contrast, implicitly control perturbation energy and are therefore substantially more robust at preserving SAE behavior. We further reveal a consistent structural vulnerability across all pruning methods: middle layers are significantly more sensitive to pruning than early or late layers. Guided by this insight, we propose a layer-wise sparsity allocation strategy, achieving lower perplexity under the same average pruning sparsity. Experiments across four model architectures validate our theoretical findings. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/osu-srml/sae-robustness-under-pruning/tree/main.

</details>


### [98] [Spectral Allocation: Why Muon Outperforms Adam, and How to Improve Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25990)
*Xiaodong Wu,Wenyi Yu,Chao Zhang,Philip Woodland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过跨样本谱探测分析揭示了正交优化器（如Muon）相对Adam加速Transformer预训练的机制，发现损失景观的谱轮廓各向异性且稳定：一个处于Edge-of-Stability的"头部"需要小步长，而"体部"可容忍大步长。基于此发现提出Spectral-Aware Muon（SAMuon），通过静态谱先验放大体部步长，在124M到1B参数的模型中相比Muon减少13.3%-24.0%的训练token达到相同验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon等正交优化器相比Adam能显著加速大规模语言模型预训练，但其加速机制尚未被完整理解。作者希望深入探究这一机制，并基于对机制的洞察进一步改进现有优化策略。

Method: 1) 在真实训练轨迹的检查点上，对每个动量缓冲进行奇异值分解，并在留出数据上估计沿每个奇异方向的损失最优步长，构建"谱剖面"；2) 通过谱剖面发现头部与体部的差异化步长需求；3) 提出SAMuon，使用低秩随机SVD精确跟随测量谱剖面，以及SAMuon-lite用秩一幂迭代实现两级近似；4) 两种方法均不引入额外持久优化器状态，在标准假设下保持Muon的渐近收敛率。

Result: 谱剖面在所有优化器和模型规模下一致：处于Edge-of-Stability的头部需要远小于体部的步长。SAMuon在124M至1B参数的"modded-nanogpt"模型中全面超越调优后的AdamW和Muon基线，比Muon少13.3%-24.0%的训练token即达到相同验证损失；SAMuon-lite以近乎零的墙钟开销保留了大部分增益。

Conclusion: 谱剖面分析为理解Muon优于Adam、Adam优于SGD提供了一个统一的谱分配解释，同时揭示了Muon均匀缩放的局限——体部未被充分利用。SAMuon通过静态谱先验放大体部步长，在保持Muon收敛保证的前提下实现了显著的训练效率提升。

Abstract: Orthogonal optimisers such as Muon can substantially accelerate large language model pretraining relative to Adam, yet the mechanism remains incompletely understood. We investigate this through an out-of-sample spectral probing analysis of Transformer loss landscapes. At checkpoints along real training trajectories, we decompose each momentum buffer into its singular directions and estimate the loss-optimal step size along each direction on held-out data. The resulting spectral profile is anisotropic yet stable across batches and training stages, and consistent across the optimisers and model scales: a volatile head operating at the Edge-of-Stability supports a much smaller step size than the tolerant bulk, which permits substantially larger steps. This profile provides a unified spectral allocation account of why Muon outperforms Adam, which outperforms SGD. It also exposes a limitation of Muon's uniform scaling: it still underutilises the bulk. Guided by this finding, we introduce Spectral-Aware Muon (SAMuon), which holds the head at the Muon scale and amplifies the bulk using a static spectral prior. We provide two variants: the complete SAMuon follows the measured profile using a low-rank randomised SVD and the simplified SAMuon-lite uses a two-level approximation via rank-one power iteration. Neither method adds persistent optimiser state or notable extra FLOPs beyond Muon at scale, and the idealised exact-whitening versions of both retain Muon's asymptotic convergence rate under standard assumptions. Across "modded-nanogpt" models from 124M to 1B parameters, both variants outperform tuned AdamW and Muon (Scion implementation) baselines in all evaluated model-scale and batch-size configurations. SAMuon requires 13.3% to 24.0% fewer training tokens to reach the same validation loss as Muon, while SAMuon-lite retains most of this gain with near-zero wall-clock overhead.

</details>


### [99] [DualOPSD: Adaptive Privileged Teachers for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26019)
*Yutong Chen,Guangfu Guo,Zhichao Xu,Kunpeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualOPSD，一个非对称交替框架，同时更新学生和特权教师策略。教师在学生轨迹上向更新后的学生分布移动，使监督信号更具响应性且无需额外回放。在Qwen3-8B上，avg@12相对OPSD在AIME 2024、AIME 2025和HMMT 2025上分别提升23.61、13.89和10.00分；收益与模型规模相关，且在所有规模下均减少截断，4B诊断显示教师与学生之间的KL散度更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD在训练过程中保持特权教师固定，而学生的分布和输出风格不断变化，导致监督信号逐渐过时。已有的OPSD方法无法适应这种分布漂移，需要让监督信号对学习者的变化更具响应性。

Method: 提出DualOPSD非对称交替训练框架：学生先从特权教师学习，然后教师在同一条学生轨迹上向更新后的学生分布移动。该更新使后续监督响应学习者，且不需要额外rollout。教师与学生为非对称交替更新的双策略框架。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B非思考模式下，avg@12相对OPSD在AIME 2024提升23.61点、AIME 2025提升13.89点、HMMT 2025提升10.00点。1.7B和4B规模的结果显示精度增益依赖模型规模。在所有三个规模下DualOPSD均减少截断。4B诊断显示教师和学生之间两个方向的KL散度均更低。

Conclusion: DualOPSD通过同时适应学生和教师策略，有效解决了OPSD中特权教师固定导致的监督信号过时问题，在多种规模上提升推理准确率、减少截断并提高教师与学生之间的分布一致性，验证了交替适应策略的有效性。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) uses a privileged copy of the student model to provide dense supervision without an external teacher. OPSD keeps this privileged teacher fixed, even though the student distribution and output style change during training. We propose DualOPSD, an asymmetric alternating framework that adapts both policies. The student first learns from the privileged teacher. The teacher then moves toward the updated student distribution on the same student trajectory. This update makes later supervision responsive to the learner and does not require another rollout. On Qwen3-8B in non-thinking mode, DualOPSD improves avg@12 over OPSD by 23.61, 13.89, and 10.00 points on AIME 2024, AIME 2025, and HMMT 2025. Results at 1.7B and 4B show that the accuracy gain depends on model scale. Across all three scales, DualOPSD reduces truncation. The 4B diagnostic also shows lower KL in both directions between the teacher and student.

</details>


### [100] [Robust CurveMoE: Multi-Norm Adversarial Defense for Mixture-of-Experts Models via Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26043)
*Xu Zhang,Ren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Robust CurveMoE，一种高效的混合专家框架，通过低损失路径连接针对不同扰动范数特化的模型，利用部分更新和选择性专家化策略降低训练成本，在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上显著提升Union准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多范数对抗防御中，现有方法通常在单一参数配置下优化相互竞争的鲁棒性目标，导致高昂的训练成本和不利的鲁棒性权衡。

Method: 1) 通过鲁棒性约束的曲线位置派生干净和范数特化的专家；2) 仅对影响力高的层进行专家化，其余参数在路由路径间共享；3) 引入贡献引导的部分更新，利用初始化梯度分数选择影响大的曲线参数，降低曲线构建成本；4) 对部分与全部曲线优化之间的目标差距提供了理论界。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet-100上，使用WideResNet和Vision Transformer架构，Robust CurveMoE在干净准确率、范数特化准确率和Union准确率上一致优于MSD和ERMC，Union准确率分别提升2.37和2.13个百分点。

Conclusion: Robust CurveMoE通过曲线路径连接范数特化模型、选择性专家化和部分参数更新的组合策略，有效缓解了多范数防御中的鲁棒性权衡问题，同时大幅降低训练开销。

Abstract: Multi-norm adversarial defense aims to protect neural networks against perturbations defined by different norm constraints, but existing methods typically optimize competing robustness objectives within a single parameter configuration, leading to substantial training cost and unfavorable robustness trade-offs. We propose Robust CurveMoE, an efficient mixture-of-experts framework that connects models specialized for different perturbation norms through a low-loss path and exploits the complementary robustness profiles of models along this path. Robust CurveMoE derives clean and norm-specialized experts from robustness-constrained curve locations and selectively expertizes only influential layers, while sharing the remaining parameters across routing paths. To further reduce curve-construction cost, we introduce contribution-guided partial updating, which selects influential curve parameters using initialization-based gradient scores. We also theoretically bound the objective gap between partial and full curve optimization. Experiments on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100 with WideResNet and Vision Transformer architectures show that Robust CurveMoE consistently improves clean, norm-specific, and Union accuracy over MSD and ERMC. In particular, it improves Union accuracy by 2.37 and 2.13 percentage points over the strongest baseline on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet-100, respectively. Extensive ablations further validate the effectiveness of partial updating, selective expertization, and robustness-constrained expert selection.

</details>


### [101] [How Much Rank Does LoRA Need? Rank-Error Bounds for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26052)
*Gerard Conangla Planes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Transformer注意力中的低秩适配（LoRA）更新提供了任务依赖的近似误差理论。作者为每个LoRA秩r推导了最小的期望KL误差的上下界，该界依赖于目标更新的尾部能量谱（downstream-weighted tail energy），并扩展到多头融合LoRA及联合查询/键更新的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA更新矩阵的秩选择通常依赖经验调参，缺乏理论指导。作者希望为注意力头下的LoRA秩选择提供严格的理论依据，理解不同下游任务中如何决定最优秩。

Method: 作者固定预训练注意力头、目标注意力函数和下游任务的输入分布，从理论上刻画秩为r的查询LoRA更新可实现的最小期望KL误差。主要工具包括：利用ψ函数（ψ(t)=min{t²,t}）刻画下界；构造无条件上界；在可实现性、几何和矩条件under推导尾部能量T_r的谱界；分析softmax饱和现象导致匹配注意力函数所需秩小于匹配有限logits所需秩；并将分析推广到多头融合LoRA和联合查询/键更新。

Result: 建立了秩r LoRA更新在Transformer注意力上的近似误差上下界：下界与ψ(‖d‖₂)成正比，无条件上界为min{‖d‖₂²/4, √2‖d‖₂}；在可实现性等条件下，最优秩r误差介于ψ(√T_r)的显式倍数和min{T_r/4, √2T_r}之间。还给出了目标Fisher界、无约束下界，并揭示了softmax饱和使匹配注意力函数所需秩更小的现象。

Conclusion: 该工作为LoRA秩选择提供了首个任务依赖的理论保障，展示了谱尾部能量在决定所需秩中的核心作用，并指出softmax饱和、多头秩共享及查询/键联合因子化约束对近似误差的影响，为实践中秩的超参数选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Choosing the rank of a low-rank adaptation (LoRA) update is usually an empirical task. In this paper, we provide a task-dependent theory of the approximation error achievable at each LoRA rank for Transformer attention. We fix a pretrained attention head, a target attention function, and a distribution over inputs from the downstream task, and bound the smallest expected Kullback--Leibler (KL) error achievable by a rank-$r$ query LoRA update. When target attention probabilities are bounded away from zero, we prove a lower bound of the error proportional to $ψ(\|d\|_2)$, where $d$ is the difference between candidate and target attention scores and $ψ(t)=\min\{t^2,t\}$. We also prove an unconditional upper bound $\min\{\|d\|_2^2/4,\sqrt2\|d\|_2\}$. Under explicit realizability, geometry, and moment conditions, we then bound the best rank-$r$ error between an explicit multiple of $ψ(\sqrt{T_r})$ and $\min\{T_r/4,\sqrt{2T_r}\}$, where $T_r$ is the downstream-weighted tail energy of the target update. We also provide target-Fisher bounds when candidate scores remain within a fixed range of the target scores, and an unrestricted lower bound when a subset of tokens carries most of the probability mass. These spectral bounds describe finite-score approximation. We then construct explicit families in which softmax saturation makes the rank required to match the attention function strictly smaller than the rank required to match the finite logits. Finally, we extend the analysis to fused multi-head LoRA and joint query/key updates, exposing the effects of rank sharing and query/key factorization constraints.

</details>


### [102] [Group-Shared Low-Rank Approximation for Mobile-Efficient Pointwise Convolutions in Large-Kernel CNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26069)
*Hao Luo,Yiting Yang,Wenyi Zhao,Man Jiang,Zhijun Lin,Ghulam Mohiuddin,Ting Jiang,Kunming Luo,Zihao Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Guoqing Wang,Wei Dong,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Channel Group-Shared (CGS)低秩近似方法，这是一种基于SVD分解的参数共享策略，用于解决大核CNN中pointwise卷积占据主导参数（>87%）导致的边缘部署存储瓶颈问题。CGS通过跨通道组共享下/上投影矩阵，并配合通道组特异的对角矩阵，在保持竞争力的同时显著减少参数量，使RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK等大核CNN能够在智能手机等资源受限边缘设备上可行部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大核CNN虽通过扩大感受野在视觉任务中表现出色，但其参数呈二次增长严重阻碍存储高效的边缘部署。现有高效架构（如深度可分离卷积）虽通过低秩近似和权重共享压缩depthwise卷积，但忽略了pointwise卷积才是参数主导（占RepLKNet-31B超87%），成为资源受限边缘设备的主要部署瓶颈，导致存储成本过高和内存加载受限。

Method: 提出CGS低秩近似策略：构建与SVD分解同构的结构化低秩范式，包含层内跨通道组共享的高参数成本下/上投影矩阵，以及通道组特异的低参数成本可缩放对角矩阵。通过这种分组共享设计实现参数的大幅压缩，同时保持模型性能。

Result: 在RepLKNet、ConvNeXt、SLaK等大核CNN上的大量实验表明，CGS增强后的模型在竞争性性能与显著降低的存储成本之间取得了有利平衡。通过缓解存储约束、降低加载时的内存带宽压力并最小化模型加载延迟，CGS使预训练大核CNN模型能够在边缘设备上可行部署。

Conclusion: CGS通过针对pointwise卷积这一被忽视的参数瓶颈，采用SVD同构的分组共享低秩策略，弥合了高性能视觉模型与实用边缘部署之间的差距，为大核CNN在智能手机等资源受限设备上的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large-kernel Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) deliver remarkable performance in vision tasks by significantly expanding receptive fields, yet their quadratic parameter growth critically impedes storage-efficient edge deployment. While existing efficient architectures adopt parameter-efficient depthwise separable convolution backbones that leverage techniques like low-rank approximation and weight sharing to compress depthwise convolutions, we identify a critical oversight: pointwise convolutions dominate parameter volume (>87% in models like RepLKNet-31B) and constitute the primary deployment bottleneck on resource-constrained edge devices. This results in prohibitive storage costs and severe memory-loading constraints on resource-limited devices (e.g., smartphones with 4-12 GB Random Access Memory (RAM)). To overcome this, we propose Channel Group-Shared (CGS) low-rank approximation, a novel Singular Value Decomposition (SVD)-based parameter-sharing strategy. CGS constructs a structured low-rank paradigm isomorphic to SVD decomposition, comprising shared (high-parameter-cost) down/up-projection matrices across channel groups within a layer and channel-group-specific (low-parameter-cost) scalable diagonal matrices. This group-sharing design achieves significant parameter reduction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that large-kernel CNNs (RepLKNet, ConvNeXt, SLaK) enhanced with CGS strike an empirically favorable balance between competitive performance and substantially reduced storage costs. Crucially, by alleviating storage constraints, reducing memory bandwidth pressure during loading, and minimizing model loading latency, CGS enables the feasible deployment of pre-trained large-kernel CNN models on edge devices, thereby bridging the gap between high-performance vision models and practical edge deployment.

</details>


### [103] [ICON Decomposition: Multivariate Concept-Level Explanations of Deep Representations for Model Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26083)
*Roshan Prakash Rane,Marco Simnacher,Manuel Pfeuffer,Marc-Andre Schulz,Nys Tjade Siegel,Maximilian Dreyer,Frederik Pahde,Wojciech Samek,Sonja Greven,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ICON分解方法，用于量化神经网络层中每个概念在控制其他概念和预测结果后所解释的方差比例，从而更准确地识别模型真正依赖的概念，避免因概念间相关性导致的虚假捷径检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于概念的可解释性方法通过独立测试每个概念能否从网络层中解码来筛查捷径学习，但这种方法忽略了概念之间的相关性，可能错误地将概念间的相关关系视为模型确实使用了该概念的证据。

Method: ICON分解通过方差分解的方式，计算每个概念在控制所有其他概念和预测结果之后所解释的层方差比例，从而衡量每个概念的独立贡献。在合成数据上对比七种基线方法，并在真实医学影像模型（皮肤病变和脑成像）上进行验证。

Result: 在具有已知真实标签的合成数据上，ICON比七种替代基线方法更准确地恢复概念重要性；在皮肤病变和脑成像模型上，它能够隔离模型真正依赖的概念，量化未被任何给定概念解释的表示，并产生稀疏解释，这些解释通过重新训练和分布外测试得到验证。

Conclusion: ICON分解为捷径学习中概念重要性评估提供了更可靠的方法，能够区分概念的真实因果贡献与相关性混淆，并通过实证验证其有效性。

Abstract: Deep neural networks often exploit spurious associations in their training data, a failure known as shortcut learning. Concept-based explainability methods screen for shortcuts by testing whether concepts such as a patient's sex or scanner settings can be decoded from a network layer. Because each concept is evaluated in isolation, these methods can mistake correlations between concepts as evidence that the model uses them. We introduce ICON decomposition, which instead quantifies how much of a layer's variance each concept explains after accounting for all other concepts and the outcome. On synthetic data with known ground truth, ICON recovers concept importance more accurately than seven alternative baseline methods. On skin-lesion and brain-imaging models, it isolates the concepts on which a model genuinely relies, quantifies the representation unexplained by any of the supplied concepts, and yields sparse explanations that we validate by retraining and out-of-distribution testing.

</details>


### [104] [TraceML: An Empirical Analysis of Human-Agent Planning in Machine Learning Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26086)
*Jiarui Yan,Weiwei Sun,Sijie Li,Wenhan Li,Yiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TraceML，一个将人类与AI智能体在相同机器学习竞赛上的开发轨迹进行配对并统一到版本级schema下的数据集与研究框架。通过分析4,465条人类Kaggle轨迹与配对智能体轨迹，作者将LLM在ML开发任务上的人类-智能体差距具体化：人类专家会多样化地进行数据工作、验证、模型修改和集成，并会回退到已放弃的方案；而智能体则陷入狭窄循环（Codex反复调整集成与提交，MLEvolve在本地变异模型）。作者进一步证明，从人类实践中提炼的规划提示可部分缩小差距，但只能影响可由指令表述的部分，努力形态仍保持智能体特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的结果导向基准（outcome-based benchmarks）虽然记录了LLM在自主机器学习开发上与人类的差距，但只对最终提交评分而丢弃了开发过程，因此无法解释差距产生的原因。本文动机在于提供一个过程级（process-level）的、版本粒度的轨迹数据，以具体化和诊断人类专家与AI智能体在ML开发行为上的差异。

Method: 构建TraceML数据集：将人类Kaggle竞赛工作与两种智能体脚手架（Codex和MLEvolve）的工作统一到版本级schema下，每个代码版本记录分数、时间戳，并标注行动类型、意图、编辑规模及分数效应。在134个竞赛中收集4,465条人类轨迹，其中7个竞赛同时由两个智能体参与，形成430条配对人类轨迹和207条智能体轨迹。作者对轨迹进行行为模式分析和对比，并设计基于人类实践的规划提示（planning prompt）来干预智能体行为。

Result: 分析发现：人类专家交替进行数据工作、验证、模型更改与集成，且会回退到已放弃的方法；而两个智能体分别陷入狭窄循环——Codex反复进行集成加权和提交调优，MLEvolve在本地变异模型，且两者既不以人类频率转换方向，也不重新开启已放弃的工作。加入从人类实践提炼的规划提示后，其所指定的行为向人类画像靠拢并提升分数，但整体努力分布仍呈智能体形态，说明指令只能弥合可归结为指令的那部分差距。

Conclusion: 结局导向的基准掩盖了人类与AI智能体在ML开发上的根本行为差异；过程级轨迹刻画揭示出智能体的策略收敛与人类探索多样性之间的鸿沟。指令型提示能部分缩小差距但无法改变智能体的整体行为轮廓，暗示进一步提升需要超越提示工程的机制。作者开源了语料、schema、标注器和提取流水线以供社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Large language models write correct code for isolated problems but remain far weaker at autonomous machine-learning development, where an agent must revise data pipelines, models, and validation over hours of feedback, and on most competitions still finishes below strong human competitors. Outcome-based benchmarks record this gap but not its cause, because they grade the final submission and discard the development process behind it. We introduce TraceML, which pairs human and agent work on the same competitions under one version-level schema: 4,465 human Kaggle trajectories across 134 competitions, seven of which are also worked by two agent scaffolds, giving 430 paired human and 207 agent trajectories. Every code version carries its score, its timestamp, and labels for the action taken, its intent, the edit size, and the score effect. Read this way, the gap becomes concrete. Experts alternate data work, validation, model changes, and ensembling, and return to approaches they had set aside. Each agent scaffold instead collapses into a narrow loop: Codex spends its steps re-weighting ensembles and tuning submissions, MLEvolve mutates its model in place, and neither pivots at the human rate nor reopens abandoned work. A short planning prompt distilled from human practice moves the behaviors it names toward the human profile and lifts scores, but the effort profile stays agent-shaped: instruction closes only the part of the gap that reduces to instructions. We release the corpus, the schema, the labelers, and the extraction pipeline at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jerryyan/TraceML.

</details>


### [105] [Agentic Autoresearch for Cell-Edge Power Control: Radically Redefining the Researcher's Role](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26093)
*Ahmad Khan,Akram Bin Sediq,Sara Azadegi Naeini,Raviraj S. Adve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文展示了将无线资源管理的机器学习算法设计（包括架构、损失函数、训练配方）完全交给自主智能体的可能性。采用autoresearch协议，AI编码智能体自主编辑训练脚本并依据单一不可变指标决定保留或丢弃改动。在81次无人值守实验中，智能体在26小时内达到收敛的MM参考的99.5%性能，推理成本降低约600倍，并发现可证明的结构而非调参常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，为无线资源管理设计机器学习算法高度依赖人工：网络架构、损失函数和训练流程均需手工指定，这一过程劳动密集且难以规模化。作者希望证明这一设计层面可以完全让渡给自主智能体，从而消除资源管理算法设计的人力瓶颈。

Method: 采用autoresearch协议：AI编码智能体编辑训练脚本、运行固定预算实验，并根据单一不可变指标保留或丢弃改动。智能体被授权控制架构家族、输入表示、输出参数化、损失函数和任务采样律。目标设定为多小区网络的sum-least-percentile-rate功率控制（非凸、非光滑、强NP难）。通过哈希固定的评估器、强制推理契约和预注册的证伪器保证结果可信。

Result: 在81次无人值守实验（26小时）中，智能体在一次固定成本推理中达到收敛的minorization-maximization参考的99.5%性能，推理成本降低约600倍，从首个工作架构起缩小了94%的差距。一个参数集适用于所有网络规模和百分位目标。它发现的输出参数化在最小百分位处精确复现了max-min最优分配，对训练权重的每个值均成立，恢复了可证明的结构而非调优常数。

Conclusion: 自主智能体能够完全接管无线资源管理算法的设计层，不仅达到接近参考算法的性能且大幅降低推理成本，还发现了具有可证明性质的通用结构。这表明机器学习算法设计本身可以被自动化，为无线资源管理乃至更广泛的算法设计领域开辟了新的范式。

Abstract: Designing machine learning algorithms for wireless resource management is labour-intensive: the architecture, the loss function and the training recipe are all specified by hand. We demonstrate that this design layer can be surrendered to an autonomous agent in its entirety. We adopt the autoresearch protocol, in which an AI coding agent edits a training script, runs a fixed-budget experiment, and retains or discards the change according to a single immutable metric. We grant the agent authority over the architecture family, the input representation, the output parameterization, the loss function and the task-sampling law, and set it a target chosen for its difficulty: sum-least-percentile-rate power control across a multicell network. The formulation targets cell-edge throughput and is non-convex, non-smooth and strongly NP-hard away from its max-min vertex. Safeguards render the results trustworthy: a hash-pinned evaluator, an enforced inference contract and a pre-registered falsifier per experiment. In eighty-one unattended experiments over twenty-six hours, the agent reached $99.5\%$ of a converged minorization-maximization reference in one fixed-cost inference pass, at roughly $600\times$ lower inference cost, closing $94\%$ of the gap from its first working architecture, with one parameter set serving every network size and percentile target. It recovered provable structure rather than tuned constants: the output parameterization it discovered reproduces the exact max-min-optimal allocation at the minimum percentile, for every value of the trained weights.

</details>

### [106] [Real-time closed-loop protocol to assess neural variability in temporal coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24895)
*Alberto Ayala,Angel Lareo,Pablo Varona,Francisco B. Rodriguez*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文评估了一种基于Victor-Purpura距离的自适应闭环刺激协议，用于在神经动力学中检测时间编码并对系统施加刺激，通过两项Hindmarsh-Rose模型验证实验表明该协议对噪声引起的变异性具有高度适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经活动通过具有刻板时间结构的尖峰序列传达信息，但这些序列受神经动力学的变异性影响。理解时间编码对于解码大脑通信至关重要，而实时闭环刺激是研究时间编码的有效手段，需要评估闭环协议如何适应变异性以驱动神经动力学到达期望状态。

Method: 该协议计算Victor-Purpura距离来量化神经系统产生的尖峰序列与触发模式之间的相似性；若序列与触发模式相似，则对系统施加刺激。使用Hindmarsh-Rose模型设计两项验证实验：(i) 检测时间编码并施加刺激产生短暂穿插的簇状放电；(ii) 检测混沌活动中的簇状放电起始并施加抑制性刺激使其规则化。实验中渐进注入高斯噪声以增加变异性。

Result: 该协议展现出高度的变异性适应能力，能有效实现目标动力学状态。即使在噪声逐步增加导致序列变异性增大的条件下，协议仍能准确检测目标时间编码并施加适当刺激。

Conclusion: 自适应闭环刺激可以增强在现实变异性条件下研究神经编码的实验方法学，为理解功能等效的时间编码实例及解码大脑通信提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Understanding temporal coding in neural systems is essential for decoding brain communication and advancing knowledge of neural information processing. Neural activity often conveys information through spike sequences with stereotypical temporal structures linked to specific functions. However, these sequences are subject to variability introduced by neural dynamics. Real-time closed-loop stimulation is a powerful approach to study temporal coding through adaptive control. In this work, we evaluate how a closed-loop protocol adapts to this variability to drive neural dynamics toward a desired state. It computes the Victor-Purpura distance to quantify similarity between spike sequences generated by the neural system and a triggering pattern. If the protocol determines that a neural sequence is similar to the trigger pattern, it applies stimulation to the system. This allows for an analysis of whether the system's responses are consistent and facilitates the identification of varying spike sequences that can be considered instances of the same functional temporal code. We designed two validation experiments using the Hindmarsh-Rose model: (i) detection of a temporal code and delivery of stimulation to produce brief interspersed bursts, and (ii) detection of burst onset in chaotic activity followed by inhibitory stimulation to regularize it. Gaussian noise was progressively injected to increase variability. The protocol exhibited high degree of adaptability to variability and was effective in achieving the target dynamics. The results reported in this paper suggest that adaptive closed-loop stimulation can enhance experimental methodologies for studying neural coding under realistic variability conditions.

</details>


### [107] [Can extrinsic methods reveal intrinsic structure? Complementing IIT with QStr](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24928)
*Jeremiah Hendren,Matteo Grasso,Francesco Ellia,Giulio Tononi*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文对比了整合信息理论（IIT）与质性结构范式（QStr）两种研究意识的方法论。IIT采用内在方法，认为体验的质性可归结为因果结构；QStr采用外在方法，依赖关系性表征。两者共享"体验及其结构"作为研究对象，但方法论起点和解释项不同且互补：QStr可为IIT提供处理窄质性（如颜色）的途径和新的数学工具，IIT则可为QStr提供绝对内在结构的根基。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在厘清IIT内在方法的哲学内涵，评估QStr与IIT的理论兼容性，并探索两者互补的具体方式，以促进意识研究不同范式间的整合。

Method: 论文采用概念分析方法，首先界定IIT的内在方法（绝对意义上的单一体验的内在结构）与QStr的外在方法（关系性表征），然后从解释目标（explanandum）、方法论起点和解释项（explanans）三个维度进行理论比较与兼容性评估。

Result: 作者论证QStr与IIT共享"体验及其结构"这一解释目标，但各自的方法论起点和解释项不同且互补。QStr能通过关系性方法补充IIT难以通过内省分解的窄质性（如颜色），并提供范畴论与度量几何等新数学工具；IIT则为QStr的关系性外在结构提供绝对内在结构的奠基。

Conclusion: IIT与QStr虽然方法论上分别属于内在与外在路径，但二者并非对立而是互补关系，可相互赋能：QStr拓展IIT的工具箱，IIT深化QStr的理论根基，共同推进意识科学的研究纲领。

Abstract: Integrated Information Theory (IIT) proposes that all quality is structure: the quality of every experience can be characterized as a phenomenal structure and accounted for as a causal structure. The IIT method can be called intrinsic in that, first, it starts by characterizing the intrinsic structure of a single experience in an absolute sense (not relative to other experiences) and, second, it preserves this intrinsic perspective when accounting for experience in physical terms. By contrast, the method of the Qualia Structure paradigm (QStr) can be called extrinsic in that its main tool is relational characterization, used to investigate both the quality of experience and the corresponding neural or information structures. This chapter gives conceptual clarity to IIT's intrinsic method, assesses the theoretical compatibility between QStr and IIT, and sketches concrete ways QStr can complement IIT. We argue that QStr and IIT share experience and its structure as their explanandum, while their methodological starting points and explanans are distinct yet complementary. QStr can bolster IIT's current research program by offering novel ways to approach narrow qualia (e.g., color), which are notoriously difficult to decompose through introspection. QStr can also supply IIT with new mathematical tools-for example, from category theory and metric geometry. In turn, IIT may help ground QStr's relational, extrinsic structures in absolute, intrinsic structures.

</details>


### [108] [A spinal circuit for collective coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25909)
*Laurence Picton,David Madrid,Alessandro Pazzaglia,Yutong Wang,Maria Bertuzzi,Andrea Ferrario,Alexandros Anastasiadis,Jonathan Arreguit,Pierre Fontanel,Chun-Xiao Huang,Karen Mulleners,Jianren Song,Auke Jan Ijspeert,Abdel El Manira*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了斑马鱼群体游动（schooling）行为中一个低阶脊髓感觉运动回路的关键作用。该回路通过脊髓内本体感觉神经元检测局部身体弯曲，基于曲率产生直接抑制信号，精确调控运动网络时序，编码自身体弯曲（自我中心）和邻居诱发的弯曲（异我中心）信号，使鱼与邻居尾流进行涡相位匹配。通过电生理、钙成像、光遗传和行为学分析证实，该回路是实时社会协调所必需的；神经力学模型和物理机器人实验表明该单一反馈回路足以产生涡相位匹配并降低游动能耗。破坏该回路会导致邻里失耦合并消除群体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体协调运动通常被认为源于大脑的高阶认知加工，但集体协调似乎可以从个体间快速局部互动中涌现，提示存在尚未明确的自组织（去中心化）在线协调机制。作者旨在探索这种低阶神经机制是否足以驱动群体行为的涌现。

Method: 综合运用电生理记录、钙成像、光遗传学和行为学分析研究斑马鱼脊髓回路；同时构建神经力学模型和物理机器人来验证单一反馈回路是否足以重现涡相位匹配及降低游动能耗。

Result: 发现脊髓内本体感觉神经元组成的感觉运动回路同时编码自身体弯曲（自我中心）与邻居诱导的弯曲（异我中心）信号，使鱼能够将其游动相位与邻居尾流匹配（涡相位匹配）。该单一反馈回路在神经力学模型和物理机器人中足以产生涡相位匹配并降低游动能耗；破坏该回路导致鱼之间失联并消除群体行为。

Conclusion: 研究表明，一个简单的脊髓回路即可通过局部互动动态同步个体，揭示低阶神经机制能够驱动协调群体行为涌现，为理解集体行为提供了一种去中心化的神经基础。

Abstract: The coordinated movement of animal groups is one of the most widespread social behaviors, which are generally attributed to high-order cognitive processing in the brain. Yet, collective coordination can seemingly emerge from rapid, local interactions between individuals, suggesting the existence of decentralized mechanisms of online coordination that remain to be identified. Here, we show that a low-order spinal sensorimotor circuit is required for real-time social coordination during schooling in zebrafish. Central to this circuit are intraspinal proprioceptive neurons that detect local body bending and deliver direct, curvature-based inhibition to precisely time the locomotor network. Combining electrophysiology, calcium imaging, optogenetics, and behavioral analysis, we show that this circuit encodes both self-generated (egocentric) and neighbor-induced (allocentric) body bending signals, enabling fish to match the phase of their swimming to the wakes of their neighbors (vortex phase matching). In a neuromechanical model and physical robot, this single feedback loop is sufficient to generate vortex phase matching and to lower the energetic cost of swimming. Disrupting this circuit uncouples neighboring fish and abolishes schooling behavior. These results show that a spinal circuit dynamically synchronizes individuals through simple, local interactions, revealing how low-order mechanisms can drive the emergence of coordinated group behavior.

</details>


<div id='bioRxiv.Neuroscience'></div>

# bioRxiv.Neuroscience [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Signatures of Hebbian plasticity in the nanoscale morphodynamics of cortical spines](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.27.747454v1)
*Soltanipour, M.,Nagel, A.,Willig, K. I.,Wolf, F.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文利用在体长时程纳米显微镜观察皮层树突棘，通过数据驱动的生成模型揭示树突棘形态动态变化中的协调性重构特征。研究发现虽然协调性重构在实时形态波动中占比最小，但会引起突触强度的巨大瞬时波动；然而突触强度仍存在突触特异性的长期持久成分，因为树突棘波动仅探索形态空间中的受限子区域。该结果提示基于连接组的神经回路模型需要超越单纯的棘头尺寸，结合树突棘形态动力学的生成模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突触棘的尺寸、形状及纳米级组织是预测生理性突触强度的关键参数，对基于连接组的神经回路建模至关重要。在完整大脑中棘的形态持续发生纳米尺度的重构，但这类形态动力学尚未被充分理解。作者特别关注形态变化如何以协调方式同时改变棘头和棘颈以影响突触强度，以及该协调重构是否可由脉冲时序依赖突触可塑性触发。

Method: 作者利用在体长时程纳米显微镜观察皮层树突棘，从中推断数据驱动的突触棘形态动力学生成模型。通过分析实时形态波动的各个组成部分，识别出协调性重构的明确特征，并分别评估各成分对瞬时突触强度的影响，同时考察波动的空间探索范围与长期持久性之间的关系。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 在体纳米显微镜下观察到了突触棘协调性重构的明确特征；(2) 协调性重构虽是实时形态波动中最小的组成部分，却造成瞬时突触强度的巨大波动；(3) 尽管存在这些波动，突触强度仍具有突触特异性的长期持久成分，原因是棘波动仅探索形态空间中受限的亚区域。

Conclusion: 纳米尺度的形态动力学棘重构会向皮层回路运行注入大量瞬时突触强度波动。基于连接组的神经回路模型不仅需考虑棘头尺寸以外的棘组织特征，还应补充基于数据驱动的棘形态动力学生成模型。

Abstract: The size, shape, and nanoscale organization of synaptic spines are predictive of physiological synaptic strength and thus key to connectome-based neural circuit models. In the intact brain, spine size and shape undergo continuous morphological remodeling. While these nanoscale morphodynamics are not well understood, it is clear that morphological changes that strongly impact synaptic strength should modify spine head and neck in a coordinated manner and that such coordinated remodeling can be triggered by spike-timing-dependent synaptic plasticity. Here we demonstrate unambiguous signatures of such coordinated spine remodeling from in vivo long-term nanoscopy of cortical spines. We infer data-driven generative models of synaptic spine morphodynamics. These models imply that although coordinated remodeling represents the smallest component of ongoing morphodynamic fluctuations, this component causes massive fluctuations in instantaneous synaptic strength. Despite these fluctuations, synaptic strengths exhibit a synapse-specific long-term persistent component that results from spine fluctuations exploring only a limited subregion of accessible spine morphospace. Our results suggest that nanoscale morphodynamic spine remodeling injects substantial fluctuations of instantaneous synaptic strength into the operation of cortical circuits. Connectome-based neural circuit models thus need to consider features of spine organization beyond spine head size and should be augmented by data-driven generative models of spine morphodynamics.

</details>


### [110] [Cortical TRPV1 modulates sensory processing through TRPA1-dependent mechanisms](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.24.746688v1)
*Amrutha, L.,Gharaei, S.,Sakai, K.,Arabzadeh, E.,Kheradpezhouh, E.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本文发现皮质TRPV1通道通过TRPA1依赖机制调节小鼠振动触觉感知，能够增强近阈值触觉刺激的感知灵敏度，提升初级体感皮层(vS1)中刺激的选择性和可分辨性，并通过降低正噪声相关性和改善群体活动解码来锐化感觉表征。该研究将TRPV1从传统的外周伤害感受离子通道角色拓展到中枢感觉计算领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TRPV1经典上被认为是参与疼痛感知的外周离子通道，但其在中枢皮层感觉处理中的生理作用尚不清楚。作者旨在探究皮质TRPV1是否以及如何调节触觉感知和初级体感皮层中的感觉表征。

Method: 以小鼠vS1为研究模型，通过行为学实验评估TRPV1激活对触觉感知灵敏度的影响；采用电生理记录分析vS1神经元在刺激诱发的反应特性、刺激选择性与可分辨性；在细胞层面研究TRPV1激活对锥体神经元兴奋性的影响及其TRPA1依赖性；分析群体水平上的噪声相关性和刺激解码表现。运用药理激活TRPV1并结合TRPA1干预手段验证通路机制。

Result: TRPV1激活增强了近阈值触觉刺激的感知灵敏度；在vS1中提升了刺激诱发的选择性和可分辨性；细胞层面以TRPA1依赖的方式增加了锥体神经元的兴奋性；群体层面降低了正噪声相关性并通过TRPA1依赖的群体活动变化改善刺激解码。

Conclusion: 研究鉴定出一个TRPV1-TRPA1信号机制，该机制调节皮层增益、锐化感觉表征并增强感知灵敏度，将TRPV1的作用从外周感觉传导扩展至中枢感觉计算。

Abstract: Transient receptor potential vanilloid 1 (TRPV1) is classically recognised as a peripheral ion channel involved in nociception, but its physiological role in cortical sensory processing remains poorly understood. Here, we investigated whether cortical TRPV1 modulates tactile perception and sensory representations in the vibrissal primary somatosensory cortex (vS1) of mice. TRPV1 activation enhanced perceptual sensitivity, particularly for near-threshold tactile stimuli. In vS1, this was accompanied by stimulus-evoked responses that enhanced stimulus selectivity and discriminability. At the cellular level, activating TRPV1 increased excitability of pyramidal neurons in a TRPA1-dependent manner. Within vS1, TRPV1 activation reduced positive noise correlations and improved stimulus decoding through TRPA1-dependent changes in population activity. Together, these findings identify a TRPV1-TRPA1 signalling mechanism that regulates cortical gain, sharpens sensory representations and enhances perceptual sensitivity. Our results extend the established role of TRPV1 beyond peripheral sensory transduction to central sensory computation.

</details>


### [111] [Integrative Multi-Tissue Analysis Identifies Synaptic Gene Networks Specific to Major Depressive Disorder in Women](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.08.24.746654v1)
*Arvind, A.,Vijay, V.,Goswami, M.,Patel, S.,Kavali, S.,Javadekar, A.,Acharya, K. K.,Chakravarty, S.,Dubey, N.*

Main category: bioRxiv.Neuroscience

TL;DR: 本研究对女性重度抑郁症患者（MDD）进行全血RNA测序，发现差异表达基因富集于突触组织、神经元结构和离子转运通路。通过与其他数据集比对及网络优先化分析，鉴定出7个突触相关基因（SHANK2、SHANK3、CACNG8、GPHN、PICK1、NRXN2和DNM2），并在女性慢性可变轻度应激（CVMS）小鼠模型的脑区验证了其中多个基因的表达改变，伴随行为变化和树突棘密度降低。结果显示人类血液、人脑及小鼠脑中存在共享的转录信号，但需更大规模研究验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重度抑郁症在患病率和分子特征上存在显著性别差异，先前研究已发现MDD患者死后脑组织中突触相关基因表达改变并呈现性别特异性模式，但不清楚女性MDD患者脑中观察到的转录变化是否可在外周血中检测到，以及是否在实验性应激模型中得到保守验证。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对6名女性MDD患者和4名匹配健康对照进行全血RNA测序；将差异表达基因（DEGs）与既往报道的女性特异性血液及死后脑组织转录组数据集进行比较；在雌性小鼠的慢性可变轻度应激（CVMS）模型中，对海马和前额叶皮层中筛选出的重叠突触相关基因进行表达评估。

Result: 外周血分析鉴定出富集于突触组织、神经元结构和离子转运通路差异表达基因；相当比例的DEGs与此前报道的外周血、女性MDD脑转录组数据集以及人类正常脑中特异性/富集表达的基因重叠。网络优先化鉴定出7个突触相关候选基因（SHANK2、SHANK3、CACNG8、GPHN、PICK1、NRXN2和DNM2）。在雌性CVMS模型中，多个基因在海马和/或前额叶皮层表达改变，伴随行为变化和树突棘密度降低。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了人类外周血、人脑数据集与小鼠脑组织之间共享的转录信号，支持外周血作为研究女性MDD中突触相关基因改变的可及性样本来源。但由于样本量小，需更大规模的研究进一步确证和验证这些发现。

Abstract: Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) shows marked gender differences in prevalence and molecular signatures. Transcriptomic studies of post-mortem human brain tissue have reported alterations in the expression of synapse-related genes in MDD, including gender-specific patterns. But it remains unclear whether transcriptional changes observed in the brains of women with MDD are detectable in peripheral blood and conserved in experimental stress models. Whole-blood RNA sequencing was performed in women with MDD (n = 6) and matched healthy controls (n = 4). Differentially expressed genes (DEGs) were compared with previously reported female-specific blood and post-mortem brain transcriptomic datasets where selected overlapping synapse-associated genes were evaluated in the hippocampus and prefrontal cortex of female mice exposed to Chronic Variable Mild Stress (CVMS). Peripheral blood analysis identified DEGs enriched for synaptic organization, neuronal structure, and ion transport pathways. A substantial proportion of DEGs overlapped with previously reported datasets from peripheral blood, female MDD brain transcriptomic studies, and genes showing exclusive/enriched expression in the normal human brain. Network-based prioritization identified seven synapse-associated genes (SHANK2, SHANK3, CACNG8, GPHN, PICK1, NRXN2 and DNM2) for further analysis. In the female CVMS model, several of these genes showed altered expression in the hippocampus and/or prefrontal cortex, alongside behavioural changes and reduced dendritic spine density. These findings highlight shared transcriptional signals across human blood and human brain datasets, as well as in the mouse brain. However, larger studies are required to confirm and validate these observations.

</details>

### [112] [Homo-RAG: Homology-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Cross-Species Gene Function Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25466)
*Azrin Sultana*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: Homo-RAG是一个基于大语言模型的基因功能预测框架，通过同源引导的多跳检索和证据感知排序，利用斑马鱼-人类直系同源关系从ZFIN、UniProt和PubMed获取证据，在150个查询和7,200份检索文档的评估中达到NDCG@10=0.9879和MRR=0.99的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非模式生物的基因功能注释仍具挑战性，20-70%的测序基因缺乏功能表征。传统基于同源性的方法成本高且强烈依赖高序列相似性，需要更高效可靠的基因功能预测方法。

Method: 构建Homo-RAG框架，包含三部分：(1) 利用斑马鱼与人类直系同源关系引导多跳证据获取；(2) 采用混合稠密与词法检索从ZFIN、UniProt和PubMed获取证据；(3) 设计Evidence Confidence Score (ECS)，整合语义相关性、实体匹配、直系同源信息、来源可靠性和文献关联信号来精化证据排序。

Result: 在150个查询和7,200份检索文档上，证据加权参数λ=0.50时NDCG@10达到0.9879，MRR达到0.99；99.33%的查询能检索到相关证据；80%的检索文档是查询独占的，说明证据质量补充而非替代检索相关性。

Conclusion: Homo-RAG为低研究度生物的可靠、基于证据的基因功能预测提供了实用且稳健的框架，解决了传统注释管线的重要局限，同时指出了证据特征和归因机制方面的未来改进方向。

Abstract: The functional annotation of genes in non-model organisms remains a significant challenge in computational biology, with 20-70% of sequenced genes lacking characterized functions. Traditional homology-based methods are often costly and strongly dependent on high sequence similarity. This study presents Homo-RAG, a framework for large language model-based gene function prediction that integrates homology-guided multi-hop retrieval with evidence-aware ranking. The framework exploits biological relationships between zebrafish and human orthologs to guide evidence acquisition from ZFIN, UniProt, and PubMed through hybrid dense and lexical retrieval. An Evidence Confidence Score (ECS) integrates semantic relevance, entity matching, orthology information, source reliability, and literature association signals to refine the ranking of retrieved evidence. Extensive evaluation across 150 queries and 7,200 retrieved documents shows that evidence weighting parameter of lambda=0.50 improves NDCG@10 to 0.9879 and MRR to 0.99, while retrieving relevant evidence for 99.33% of queries. Furthermore, 80% of the retrieved documents are query-exclusive, indicating that evidence quality complements rather than replaces retrieval relevance. These findings establish Homo-RAG as a practical and robust framework for reliable, evidence-grounded gene function prediction in understudied organisms. The study addresses important limitations of conventional annotation pipelines while identifying opportunities for future improvements in evidence features and attribution mechanisms.

</details>


### [113] [Synthesis of Hopfield Neural Network: Novel Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25481)
*Garimella Rama Murthy*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文基于文献[1]中提出的"以超立方体期望角落作为稳定状态来合成Hopfield神经网络"的逻辑基础，证明了无论神经元数量是奇数还是偶数，都可以将更多的超立方体角落编程为稳定状态，为Hopfield神经网络的"编程问题"提供了新视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield神经网络的"编程问题"（即如何将期望的模式设为稳定状态）是核心挑战之一。现有方法在编程超立方体角落作为稳定状态时存在数量限制，本文旨在突破这一限制，证明无论神经元奇偶性如何，都可以编程更多的角落作为稳定状态。

Method: 基于文献[1]提出的逻辑合成方法，利用超立方体期望角落作为稳定状态来构建Hopfield神经网络，并通过数学证明展示在神经元数量为偶数或奇数的情况下，均可实现更多角落的编程。

Result: 证明结果表明，无论神经元数量为偶数还是奇数，Hopfield神经网络都可以将更多的超立方体角落编程为稳定状态，突破了以往的编程数量限制。

Conclusion: 该研究为Hopfield神经网络的"编程问题"提供了新的理论视角，扩展了可编程稳定状态的数量范围，对未来网络设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: Using the logical basis of synthesizing Hopfield Neural Network with desired corners of hypercube as stable states (proposed in [1]), it is proved that more corners of hypercube can be programmed as stable states (whether the number of neurons is even or odd). The research paper presents a new perspective to the so called "Programming Problem" of Hopfield Neural Network.

</details>

### [114] [On the Role of Dispersion in One Model of Propagation of Elastic Excitations in Nerves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25408)
*Alexander I. Kozlov*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究描述神经活动电位传播过程中膜密度变化的非线性微分方程，根据方程的色散项取值，分析了精确周期解和孤立子解的存在性及其形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经活动电位的传播是神经科学中的核心问题，其背后涉及非线性动力学机制。理解膜密度在电位传播过程中的变化规律，对于揭示神经信号传导的物理本质具有重要的理论和实际意义。

Method: 对描述神经中活动电位传播过程中膜密度变化的非线性微分方程进行解析研究，通过考察方程中色散项的不同取值，系统地分析精确周期解和孤立子解是否存在及其不同的表现形式。

Result: 研究表明，该非线性微分方程的解的形式（包括精确周期解和孤立子解）与方程中色散项的参数取值密切相关，色散项的不同取值决定了解的存在性与具体形态。

Conclusion: 该方程的解的性质由色散项控制，色散参数的变化会导致精确周期解和孤立子解的存在性与形式发生改变，这为理解神经信号传播的非线性动力学特性提供了数学基础。

Abstract: The nonlinear differential equation, which describes changes of membrane's density in the process of propagation of active potential along a nerve, has been studied. Depending on values of dispersion terms of that equation, forms and possible existence of exact periodic and solitary solutions were considered.

</details>


### [115] [Where Energy Is Spent Sets the Depth of Kinetic Proofreading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25748)
*Uğur Çetiner*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过拓扑学分析揭示了动力学校对（kinetic proofreading）中能量消耗位置对精度极限的根本限制：驱动级联中的第j层时，误差下限为ε_eq^(K-j+1)，该指数等于下游子图的独立循环数加一；驱动第一层可调控全部K个检查点，而驱动最后一层仅能将误差降到ε_eq^2。论文还推导了误差对驱动强度的精确依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动力学校对理论认为能量驱动可以购买任意高的精度，但本文作者发现能量消耗的具体位置决定了精度所能达到的上限，提出了一个尚未被研究的关键问题：驱动放置如何拓扑地限制非平衡误差校正的能力。

Method: 运用代数拓扑工具（第一Betti数）刻画网络循环结构，结合K检查点级联模型的动力学分析，从理论上推导驱动第j层时的误差下界，并精确求解误差对驱动强度的函数依赖关系。

Result: 驱动第j层时误差下界为ε_eq^(K-j+1)，该指数由下游子图的独立循环数（第一Betti数加1）决定；驱动首层可实现ε_eq^K的最优精度，驱动末层仅限ε_eq^2；误差随驱动强度要么单调递减，要么存在唯一全局最小值，超过该值后续增强驱动反而降低精度。

Conclusion: 驱动位置在拓扑层面决定了非平衡误差校正的精度极限，能量在哪里消耗决定了这些能量能买到多少精度，这为理解生物化学网络的校对机制提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Kinetic proofreading buys accuracy with energy. Where the energy is spent, we show, caps how much accuracy it can buy. Drive layer $j$ of a $K$-checkpoint cascade and the error never falls below $\eeq^{K-j+1}$, where $\eeq$ is the error the same network reaches at equilibrium. The exponent is one plus the first Betti number of the downstream subgraph, which counts its independent cycles. Driving the first layer can recruit all $K$ checkpoints, whereas driving the final layer restricts the best possible scaling to $\eeq^2$, independently of network depth. We also derive the exact dependence on driving strength and show that the error either decreases monotonically or has a unique global minimum, beyond which stronger driving worsens accuracy. Drive placement therefore imposes a topological limit on nonequilibrium error correction.

</details>
